A budding yeast that is homozygous recessive for <span style="color: #a33026;">Gene M</span> colonies are small, dense, and secrete compounds that inhibit the growth of nearby colonies.</li> <li><strong><span style="color: #176a72;">Gene Y</span></strong> is analogous to the '<span style="color: #176a72;"><i>yolk</i></span>' phenotype. The yeast <i>Saccharomyces cerevisiae</i> is used in this study. A cross has been performed to study the linkage relationships among two genes, and the resulting genotypes are summarized in the table below.</p><h6>Characteristics of Recessive Phenotypes</h6><p><ul><li><strong><span style="color: #825200;">Gene M</span></strong> is connected with the '<span style="color: #825200;"><i>militant</i></span>' phenotype. A budding yeast that is homozygous recessive for <span style="color: #825200;">Gene M</span> colonies are small, dense, and secrete compounds that inhibit the growth of nearby colonies.</li> <li><strong><span style="color: #176a72;">Gene X</span></strong> is associated with the '<span style="color: #176a72;"><i>xenon</i></span>' phenotype. A budding yeast that is homozygous recessive for <span style="color: #176a72;">Gene X</span> cells emit a faint glow under UV light, as if they were fluorescent.</li> </ul></p><table style="border-collapse: collapse; MC	<p>0978_142b</p> <h5>Unordered Tetrad Two Gene Mapping</h5><p>In this problem, you will use unordered tetrads to determine the between a single pair of genes and calculate the distances between them. The yeast <i>Saccharomyces cerevisiae</i> is used in this study. A cross has been performed to study the linkage relationships among two genes, and the resulting genotypes are summarized in the table below.</p><h6>Characteristics of Recessive Phenotypes</h6><p><ul><li><strong><span style="color: #16629b;">Gene P</span></strong> is associated with the '<span style="color: #16629b;"><i>pebble</i></span>' phenotype. A budding yeast that is homozygous recessive for <span style="color: #16629b;">Gene P</span> produces colonies with a rough, uneven surface that resembles a collection of tiny pebbles.</li> <li><strong><span style="color: #9d2f55;">Gene W</span></strong> is correlated with the '<span style="color: #9d2f55;"><i>webbed</i></span>' phenotype. A budding yeast that is homozygous recessive for <span style="color: #9d2f55;">Gene W</span> colonies produce delicate, web-like strands that connect neighboring colonies in a cobweb pattern.</li> </ul></p><table style="border-collapse: collapse;
